 =  109.4945

Notes and Assumptions:
1)  LPP proactive replacement capital expenditures in base rates total $77.375 million in CY 2017, $87.189 million in CY 2018 and $88.933 million in CY 2019.   
      Revenue requirement assumes capital expenditures are placed in-service in the month spent
2)  Depreciation is based on the Depreciation Study recommended composite depreciation rate for mains and services based on 9/30/2015 plant balances
3)  Utilizes pre-tax WACC by calendar year
4)  The amount subject to surcharge is limited to the Company's average costs of main replacement for comparable pipe materials, sizes, strata (e.g., pavement, grass) and working condition
      (subject to inflation and other reasonable adjustments (e.g., new permitting requirements))
5)  The CY 2017 incremental mileage spend will be rolled forward to the CY 2018 and CY 2019 surcharges, calculating a return on the net plant investment and associated depreciation expense.
6)  The CY 2018 incremental mileage spend will be rolled forward to the CY 2019 surcharge, calculating a return on the net plant investment and associated depreciation expense.
The final revenue requirement should be adjusted to incorporate any changes to the capital investment, depreciation rates, pre-tax WACC or opex approved in this case

Notes and Assumptions:
1)  City/State capital expenditures in base rates total $180.899 million in CY 2017, $185.811 million in CY 2018 and $189.871 million in CY 2019.    
      Revenue requirement assumes capital expenditures are placed in-service in the month spent.
2)  Depreciation is based on the Depreciation Study recommended composite depreciation rate for mains and services based on 9/30/2015 plant balances.
3)  Utilizes pre-tax WACC by calendar year
4)  The CY 2017 incremental/under spend will be rolled forward to the CY 2018 and CY 2019 deferral, calculating a return on the net plant investment and associated depreciation expense. 
5)  The CY 2018 incremental/under spend will be rolled forward to the CY 2019 deferral, calculating a return on the net plant investment and associated depreciation expense. 
The final revenue requirement should be adjusted to incorporate any changes to the capital investment, depreciation rates, pre-tax WACC or opex approved in this case.
